roomsim/roomsim.cpp:120:    };

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:143:        val_t p2 = axis.dot(v2);

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:146:                  boxHalfSize.z * std::abs(axis.z);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:147:        auto [minP, maxP] = minMax3(p0, p1, p2);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:148:        return !(minP > r || maxP < -r);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:149:    };

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:323:    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:325:        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:326:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:327:        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:328:            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:329:            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:330:            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:331:            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:332:            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:333:            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:334:            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:335:            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:336:            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:337:            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:338:            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:339:            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:340:        };
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:341:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:342:        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:343:        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:344:            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:345:            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:346:            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:347:            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:348:        };
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:349:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:350:        // Subdivide
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:351:        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:352:            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:353:            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:354:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:355:            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:356:                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:357:                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:358:                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:359:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:360:                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:361:                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:362:                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:363:                vertices.push_back(mid);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:364:                midpointCache[key] = idx;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:365:                return idx;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:366:            };
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:367:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:368:            for (const auto& face : faces) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:369:                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:370:                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:371:                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:372:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:373:                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:374:                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:375:                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:376:                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:377:            }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:378:            faces = std::move(newFaces);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:379:        }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:380:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:381:        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a "room")
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:382:        triangles.reserve(faces.size());
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:383:        for (const auto& face : faces) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:384:            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:385:            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:386:        }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:387:    }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:388:};
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:389:

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:394:class RandomGenerator {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:395:    std::mt19937 rng;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:396:    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:397:public:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:398:    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:399:    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:400:};
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:401:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:402:// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:403:Vec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:404:    val_t u = rng.rand();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:405:    val_t v = rng.rand();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:406:    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:407:        u = 1.0f - u;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:408:        v = 1.0f - v;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:409:    }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:410:    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:411:    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:412:    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:413:}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:414:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:415:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:416:// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:417:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:418:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:419:val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:420:                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:421:    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:422:    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:423:    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:424:    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:425:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:426:    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:427:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:428:    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:429:    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:430:    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:431:    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:432:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:433:    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:434:    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:435:    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:436:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:437:    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:438:}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:439:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:440:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:441:// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:442:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:443:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:444:// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:445:// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:446:bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:447:                  const Octree& octree,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:448:                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:449:    Vec3 dir = to - from;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:450:    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:451:    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:452:    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:453:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:454:    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:455:        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:456:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:457:        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:458:        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:459:            return true;  // Ray is blocked
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:460:        }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:461:        return false;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:462:    });
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:463:}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:464:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:465:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:466:// Form Factor Computation (Kij)
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:467:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:468:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:469:// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:470:val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:471:    val_t vNorm = v.norm();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:472:    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:473:    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:474:}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:475:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:476:// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:477:val_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:478:                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:479:                  const Octree& octree,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:480:                  RandomGenerator& rng) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:481:    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:482:    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:483:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:484:    // Cull triangles facing the same direction
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:485:    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:486:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:487:    val_t kij = ZERO;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:488:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:489:    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:490:        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:491:        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:492:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:493:        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:494:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:495:        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:496:        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:497:        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:498:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:499:        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:500:        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:501:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:502:        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:503:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:504:        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:505:    }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:506:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:507:    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:508:}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:509:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:510:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:511:// Tau (time delay) Computation
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:512:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:513:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:514:int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:515:    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:516:    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:517:}

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:537:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:538:    size_t sourceIndex;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:539:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:540:    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:548:void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:549:                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:551:    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:552:    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:553:    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:554:    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:555:    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:556:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:557:    printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:558:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:559:    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:560:    printf("Building octree...\n");
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:561:    state.octree.build(state.triangles);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:562:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:563:    // Initialize areas
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:564:    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:565:    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:566:        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:567:    }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:568:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:569:    // Initialize reflectivity
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:570:    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:571:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:572:    // Initialize matrices
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:573:    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:574:    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:577:    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:578:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:579:    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:580:    size_t timeOn = 0;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:581:    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:582:    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:583:        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:584:    }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:585:}

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:588:// Precomputation Phase

roomsim/roomsim.cpp:590:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:591:void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:592:    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:593:    RandomGenerator rng(42);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:594:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:595:    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:596:        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:597:            if (i == j) continue;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:598:            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:599:                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:600:        }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:601:        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:602:            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:603:        }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:604:    }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:605:}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:606:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:607:void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:608:    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:609:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:610:    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:611:        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:612:            if (i == j) continue;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:613:            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:614:                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:615:        }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:616:    }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:617:}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:618:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:619:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:620:// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:621:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:622:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:623:void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:624:    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:625:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:626:    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:627:        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:628:            val_t sumB = ZERO;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:629:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:630:            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:631:                if (i == j) continue;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:632:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:633:                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:634:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:635:                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:636:                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:637:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:638:                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:639:                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:640:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:641:                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:642:                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:643:                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:644:                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:645:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:646:                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:647:                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:648:            }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:649:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:650:            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:651:            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:652:        }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:653:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:654:        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:655:            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:656:        }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:657:    }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:658:}
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:659:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:660:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:661:// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:662:// ============================================================================
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:663:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:664:void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:665:    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:666:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:667:    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:668:        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:669:        int bestT = 0;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:670:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:671:        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:672:        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:673:            val_t sum = ZERO;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:674:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:675:            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:676:                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:677:                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:678:                sum += pS * pB;
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:679:            }
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:680:
roomsim/roomsim.cpp:681:            if (sum > maxCorr) {

// ============================================================================
// Mesh Generation: Icosphere
// ============================================================================

// Generate an icosphere by subdividing an icosahedron
class IcosphereMesh {
public:
    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;

    IcosphereMesh(int subdivisions, val_t radius) {
        // Initial icosahedron vertices
        const val_t t = (1.0f + std::sqrt(5.0f)) / 2.0f;

        std::vector<Vec3> vertices = {
            Vec3(-1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 1,  t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3(-1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 1, -t,  0).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0, -1,  t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0,  1,  t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0, -1, -t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( 0,  1, -t).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3( t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3(-t,  0, -1).normalized() * radius,
            Vec3(-t,  0,  1).normalized() * radius
        };

        // Initial icosahedron faces (20 triangles)
        std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> faces = {
            {0, 11, 5}, {0, 5, 1}, {0, 1, 7}, {0, 7, 10}, {0, 10, 11},
            {1, 5, 9}, {5, 11, 4}, {11, 10, 2}, {10, 7, 6}, {7, 1, 8},
            {3, 9, 4}, {3, 4, 2}, {3, 2, 6}, {3, 6, 8}, {3, 8, 9},
            {4, 9, 5}, {2, 4, 11}, {6, 2, 10}, {8, 6, 7}, {9, 8, 1}
        };

        // Subdivide
        for (int i = 0; i < subdivisions; ++i) {
            std::vector<std::array<idx_t, 3>> newFaces;
            std::map<std::pair<idx_t, idx_t>, idx_t> midpointCache;

            auto getMidpoint = [&](idx_t i1, idx_t i2) -> idx_t {
                auto key = std::make_pair(std::min(i1, i2), std::max(i1, i2));
                auto it = midpointCache.find(key);
                if (it != midpointCache.end()) return it->second;

                Vec3 mid = (vertices[i1] + vertices[i2]) / 2.0f;
                mid = mid.normalized() * radius;
                idx_t idx = static_cast<idx_t>(vertices.size());
                vertices.push_back(mid);
                midpointCache[key] = idx;
                return idx;
            };

            for (const auto& face : faces) {
                idx_t a = getMidpoint(face[0], face[1]);
                idx_t b = getMidpoint(face[1], face[2]);
                idx_t c = getMidpoint(face[2], face[0]);

                newFaces.push_back({face[0], a, c});
                newFaces.push_back({face[1], b, a});
                newFaces.push_back({face[2], c, b});
                newFaces.push_back({a, b, c});
            }
            faces = std::move(newFaces);
        }

        // Build triangles (normals pointing inward for a "room")
        triangles.reserve(faces.size());
        for (const auto& face : faces) {
            // Reverse winding to make normals point inward
            triangles.emplace_back(vertices[face[2]], vertices[face[1]], vertices[face[0]]);
        }
    }
};

// ============================================================================
// Random Number Generation
// ============================================================================

class RandomGenerator {
    std::mt19937 rng;
    std::uniform_real_distribution<val_t> dist;
public:
    explicit RandomGenerator(uint32_t seed = 42) : rng(seed), dist(0.0f, 1.0f) {}
    val_t rand() { return dist(rng); }
};

// Generate a random point inside a triangle using barycentric coordinates
Vec3 randomPointInTriangle(const Triangle& t, RandomGenerator& rng) {
    val_t u = rng.rand();
    val_t v = rng.rand();
    if (u + v > 1.0f) {
        u = 1.0f - u;
        v = 1.0f - v;
    }
    Vec3 ab = t.b - t.a;
    Vec3 ac = t.c - t.a;
    return t.a + ab * u + ac * v;
}

// ============================================================================
// Ray-Triangle Intersection (Möller-Trumbore algorithm)
// ============================================================================

val_t rayTriangleIntersect(const Vec3& orig, const Vec3& dir,
                            const Vec3& v0, const Vec3& v1, const Vec3& v2) {
    Vec3 e1 = v1 - v0;
    Vec3 e2 = v2 - v0;
    Vec3 pvec = dir.cross(e2);
    val_t det = e1.dot(pvec);

    if (std::abs(det) < EPSILON) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    val_t invDet = 1.0f / det;
    Vec3 tvec = orig - v0;
    val_t u = tvec.dot(pvec) * invDet;
    if (u < 0.0f || u > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    Vec3 qvec = tvec.cross(e1);
    val_t v = dir.dot(qvec) * invDet;
    if (v < 0.0f || u + v > 1.0f) return std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

    return e2.dot(qvec) * invDet;
}

// ============================================================================
// Visibility Testing (Octree-accelerated)
// ============================================================================

// Check if a ray between two triangles is blocked by any other triangle
// Uses octree for O(log N) instead of O(N) search
bool isRayBlocked(const Vec3& from, const Vec3& to,
                  const Octree& octree,
                  size_t srcTriIdx, size_t dstTriIdx) {
    Vec3 dir = to - from;
    val_t rayLen = dir.norm();
    if (rayLen < EPSILON) return true;
    Vec3 dirNorm = dir / rayLen;

    return octree.applyToTris(from, to, [&](size_t idx, const Triangle& tri) {
        if (idx == srcTriIdx || idx == dstTriIdx) return false;

        val_t dist = rayTriangleIntersect(from, dirNorm, tri.a, tri.b, tri.c);
        if (dist > EPSILON && dist < rayLen - EPSILON) {
            return true;  // Ray is blocked
        }
        return false;
    });
}

// ============================================================================
// Form Factor Computation (Kij)
// ============================================================================

// Compute the cosine of angle between vector and triangle normal
val_t cosPhi(const Vec3& v, const Vec3& normal) {
    val_t vNorm = v.norm();
    if (vNorm <= EPSILON) return ZERO;
    return std::max(ZERO, v.dot(normal) / vNorm);
}

// Compute form factor Kij between triangle i (receiver) and triangle j (emitter)
val_t computeKij(size_t idxI, size_t idxJ,
                  const std::vector<Triangle>& triangles,
                  const Octree& octree,
                  RandomGenerator& rng) {
    const Triangle& triI = triangles[idxI];
    const Triangle& triJ = triangles[idxJ];

    // Cull triangles facing the same direction
    if (triI.normal().dot(triJ.normal()) > 0.99f) return ZERO;

    val_t kij = ZERO;

    for (int r = 0; r < NUM_RAYS; ++r) {
        Vec3 pI = randomPointInTriangle(triI, rng);
        Vec3 pJ = randomPointInTriangle(triJ, rng);

        if (isRayBlocked(pI, pJ, octree, idxI, idxJ)) continue;

        Vec3 v = pJ - pI;
        val_t distSqr = v.squaredNorm();
        if (distSqr < EPSILON) continue;

        val_t cosPhiI = cosPhi(v, triI.normal());
        val_t cosPhiJ = cosPhi(-v, triJ.normal());

        if (cosPhiI <= ZERO || cosPhiJ <= ZERO) continue;

        kij += (cosPhiI * cosPhiJ) / (PI * distSqr);
    }

    return kij * INV_NUM_RAYS;
}

// ============================================================================
// Tau (time delay) Computation
// ============================================================================

int computeTau(const Triangle& triI, const Triangle& triJ) {
    val_t dist = (triI.center() - triJ.center()).norm();
    return static_cast<int>(std::ceil(dist * INV_WAVE_SPEED));
}

// ============================================================================
// Room Response Simulation State
// ============================================================================

struct SimulationState {
    size_t numTriangles;
    size_t numTimesteps;

    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Form factors (N x N matrix, row-major)
    std::vector<int> tau;           // Time delays (N x N matrix, row-major)
    std::vector<val_t> radE;        // Emission radiosity (T x N matrix)
    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Reflected radiosity (T x N matrix)
    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Computed distances from source

    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure

    size_t sourceIndex;

    size_t idx2d(size_t i, size_t j) const { return i * numTriangles + j; }
    size_t idxTN(size_t t, size_t n) const { return t * numTriangles + n; }
};

// ============================================================================
// Initialization
// ============================================================================

void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
    // Generate mesh
    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;

    printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);

    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
    printf("Building octree...\n");
    state.octree.build(state.triangles);

    // Initialize areas
    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
    }

    // Initialize reflectivity
    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);

    // Initialize matrices
    state.kij.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.tau.resize(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
    state.radE.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.radB.resize(timesteps * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.distances.resize(state.numTriangles, ZERO);

    // Set source emission (active for first half of timesteps)
    size_t timeOn = 0;
    size_t timeOff = timesteps / 2;
    for (size_t t = timeOn; t < timeOff; ++t) {
        state.radE[state.idxTN(t, state.sourceIndex)] = 1.0f;
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
    RandomGenerator rng(42);

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeKij(
                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
        }
        if ((i + 1) % 100 == 0 || i + 1 == state.numTriangles) {
            printf("  Progress: %zu/%zu triangles\n", i + 1, state.numTriangles);
        }
    }
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)] = computeTau(
                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)

// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
        for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
            val_t sumB = ZERO;

            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                if (i == j) continue;

                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(i, j)];

                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;

                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(i, j)];
                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;

                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxTN(srcTime, j)];
                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;

                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
            }

            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
            state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] = state.rho[i] * sumB + state.radE[state.idxTN(t, i)];
        }

        if ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps) {
            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;

        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                val_t pB = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt, i)];
                val_t pS = state.radB[state.idxTN(tt - t, state.sourceIndex)];
                sum += pS * pB;
            }

            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }

        state.distances[i] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    printf("\nValidation:\n");

    // Check that distances are non-negative
    bool allNonNegative = true;
    val_t minDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    val_t maxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
    int nonZeroCount = 0;

    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        val_t d = state.distances[i];
        if (d < 0) {
            allNonNegative = false;
            printf("  ERROR: Negative distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
            printf("  ERROR: Non-finite distance at triangle %zu: %f\n", i, d);
            return false;
        }
        minDist = std::min(minDist, d);
        maxDist = std::max(maxDist, d);
        sumDist += d;
        if (d > EPSILON) nonZeroCount++;
    }

    printf("  Distance range: [%.4f, %.4f]\n", minDist, maxDist);
    printf("  Average distance: %.4f\n", sumDist / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles));
    printf("  Non-zero distances: %d/%zu\n", nonZeroCount, state.numTriangles);

    // Check source distance is zero or very small
    val_t srcDist = state.distances[state.sourceIndex];
    if (srcDist > WAVE_SPEED * 2) {
        printf("  WARNING: Source triangle distance is non-zero: %.4f\n", srcDist);
    }

    // Check radiosity propagation (some triangles should have received energy)
    int receivedEnergy = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            if (state.radB[state.idxTN(t, i)] > EPSILON) {
                receivedEnergy++;
                break;
            }
        }
    }

    printf("  Triangles receiving energy: %d/%zu\n", receivedEnergy, state.numTriangles);

    if (receivedEnergy == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: No triangles received energy - simulation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

    // Check Kij matrix (should have some non-zero entries)
    int nonZeroKij = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        if (state.kij[i] > EPSILON) nonZeroKij++;
    }
    printf("  Non-zero form factors: %d/%zu (%.2f%%)\n",
           nonZeroKij, state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles,
           100.0f * nonZeroKij / static_cast<val_t>(state.numTriangles * state.numTriangles));

    if (nonZeroKij == 0) {
        printf("  ERROR: All form factors are zero - visibility computation failed\n");
        return false;
    }

    if (!allNonNegative) {
        return false;
    }

    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
    return true;
}

// ============================================================================
// Hash for Verification
// ============================================================================

uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

// ============================================================================
// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
// ============================================================================

int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
    int subdivisions = 0;
    int triangles = 20;
    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
        subdivisions++;
        triangles *= 4;
    }
    return subdivisions;
}

// ============================================================================
// Main
// ============================================================================

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }

    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);

    printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
    printf("===================================\n");
    printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
    printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
    printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
    printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    printf("\n");

    // Initialize
    SimulationState state;
    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);

    printf("\n");

    // Precomputation
    auto startPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);

    auto endPre = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto preDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endPre - startPre).count();

    printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Simulation
    auto startSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    runSimulation(state);

    auto endSim = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto simDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endSim - startSim).count();

    printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Distance computation
    auto startDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();

    computeDistances(state);

    auto endDist = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto distDuration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(endDist - startDist).count();

    printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
    printf("\n");

    // Total time
    long totalTime = preDuration + simDuration + distDuration;
    printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);

    // Performance metrics
    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

    printf("\nPerformance:\n");
    printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
    printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
    printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
    printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
    printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
    printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);

    // Memory usage
    size_t memKij = n * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t memTau = n * n * sizeof(int);
    size_t memRad = 2 * t * n * sizeof(val_t);
    size_t totalMem = memKij + memTau + memRad;
    printf("  Memory usage: %.2f MB\n", totalMem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));

    // Hash
    uint64_t hash = computeHash(state);
    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
    printf("\n");
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        // Convert distances to double for output
        std::vector<double> distData(state.distances.begin(), state.distances.end());
        print_results(distData, "Distances");
    }

    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        if (!validateResults(state)) {
            return 1;
        }
    }

    return 0;
}
