#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

codex
The code’s dominant kernels are all row-independent except for the timestep history: form factors, time delays, wave updates, and cross-correlation can be partitioned by receiver triangle. I’m using contiguous MPI row ownership, keeping only each rank’s matrix rows and timestep results, and exchanging the current radiosity row with `MPI_Allgatherv` at each timestep so the wave dependencies remain correct.

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}
+

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+long elapsedMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    return rank == 0 ? static_cast<long>(maximumSeconds * 1000.0) : 0;
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}
+

struct SimulationState {
    size_t numTriangles;
    size_t numTimesteps;

    MPI_Comm comm = MPI_COMM_WORLD;
    int rank = 0;
    int worldSize = 1;
    std::vector<int> rowCounts;
    std::vector<int> rowDisplacements;
    size_t firstTriangle = 0;
    size_t localTriangles = 0;

    std::vector<Triangle> triangles;
    std::vector<val_t> areas;       // Area of each triangle
    std::vector<val_t> rho;         // Reflectivity (0.0 to 1.0)
    std::vector<val_t> kij;         // Locally owned form-factor rows
    std::vector<int> tau;           // Locally owned time-delay rows
    std::vector<val_t> radB;        // Rolling global radiosity history
    std::vector<val_t> localRadB;   // Full history for locally owned triangles
    std::vector<val_t> sourceRadB;  // Source history, replicated on every rank
    std::vector<val_t> localDistances;
    std::vector<val_t> distances;   // Complete result on rank zero

    Octree octree;                  // Spatial acceleration structure

    size_t sourceIndex;
    size_t maxTau = 0;
    size_t historyLength = 0;

    size_t idx2d(size_t localI, size_t j) const {
        return localI * numTriangles + j;
    }
    size_t idxLocalTN(size_t t, size_t localI) const {
        return t * localTriangles + localI;
    }
    size_t idxHistory(size_t t, size_t n) const {
        return (t % historyLength) * numTriangles + n;
    }

    SimulationState() {
        MPI_Comm_rank(comm, &rank);
        MPI_Comm_size(comm, &worldSize);
    }
};

void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
                       std::vector<int>& counts,
                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));

    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
    size_t displacement = 0;
    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
        }
        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
        displacement += count;
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Initialization
// ============================================================================

void initializeSimulation(SimulationState& state, int subdivisions, size_t timesteps,
                          size_t sourceIdx, val_t reflectivity) {
    // Generate mesh
    IcosphereMesh mesh(subdivisions, 10.0f);  // Radius 10 units
    state.triangles = std::move(mesh.triangles);
    state.numTriangles = state.triangles.size();
    state.numTimesteps = timesteps;
    state.sourceIndex = sourceIdx % state.numTriangles;
    buildRowPartition(state.numTriangles, state.worldSize,
                      state.rowCounts, state.rowDisplacements);
    state.firstTriangle = static_cast<size_t>(state.rowDisplacements[state.rank]);
    state.localTriangles = static_cast<size_t>(state.rowCounts[state.rank]);

    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf("Generated icosphere mesh with %zu triangles\n", state.numTriangles);
    }

    // Build octree for spatial acceleration
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Building octree on each MPI rank...\n");
    state.octree.build(state.triangles);

    // Initialize areas
    state.areas.resize(state.numTriangles);
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        state.areas[i] = state.triangles[i].area();
    }

    // Initialize reflectivity
    state.rho.resize(state.numTriangles, reflectivity);

    // Matrix rows and target histories are distributed by receiver triangle.
    state.kij.assign(state.localTriangles * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
    state.tau.assign(state.localTriangles * state.numTriangles, 0);
    state.localRadB.assign(timesteps * state.localTriangles, ZERO);
    state.sourceRadB.assign(timesteps, ZERO);
    state.localDistances.assign(state.localTriangles, ZERO);
    if (state.rank == 0) state.distances.assign(state.numTriangles, ZERO);
}

// ============================================================================
// Precomputation Phase
// ============================================================================

void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) {
        printf("Computing form factors (Kij) with %d MPI ranks...\n", state.worldSize);
    }

    // Count the pairs which consume random numbers.  An exclusive scan gives
    // every rank its exact offset in the serial RNG stream, preserving the
    // original ray samples while distributing the expensive visibility work.
    uint64_t localRandomPairs = 0;
    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangles; ++localI) {
        const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i != j && state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
                ++localRandomPairs;
            }
        }
    }

    uint64_t randomPairOffset = 0;
    MPI_Exscan(&localRandomPairs, &randomPairOffset, 1, MPI_UINT64_T,
               MPI_SUM, state.comm);
    if (state.rank == 0) randomPairOffset = 0;

    RandomGenerator rng(42);
    rng.discard(randomPairOffset * static_cast<uint64_t>(NUM_RAYS) * 2ULL);

    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangles; ++localI) {
        const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            if (state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) > 0.99f) continue;
            state.kij[state.idx2d(localI, j)] = computeKij(
                i, j, state.triangles, state.octree, rng);
        }
    }

    if (state.rank == 0) printf("  Form factors computed across all MPI ranks\n");
}

void computeTimeDelays(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing time delays (Tau)...\n");

    int localMaxTau = 0;
    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangles; ++localI) {
        const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
            if (i == j) continue;
            const int tau = computeTau(
                state.triangles[i], state.triangles[j]);
            state.tau[state.idx2d(localI, j)] = tau;
            localMaxTau = std::max(localMaxTau, tau);
        }
    }

    int globalMaxTau = 0;
    MPI_Allreduce(&localMaxTau, &globalMaxTau, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, state.comm);
    state.maxTau = static_cast<size_t>(globalMaxTau);
    state.historyLength = state.maxTau + 1;
    state.radB.assign(state.historyLength * state.numTriangles, ZERO);
}

// ============================================================================
// Simulation Phase (Wave Propagation)
// ============================================================================

void runSimulation(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Running wave propagation simulation...\n");

    std::vector<val_t> localCurrent(state.localTriangles, ZERO);

    for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
        for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangles; ++localI) {
            const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
            val_t sumB = ZERO;

            for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
                if (i == j) continue;

                int tauij = state.tau[state.idx2d(localI, j)];

                // Skip if wave hasn't yet propagated from j to i
                if (static_cast<int>(t) < tauij) continue;

                val_t kij = state.kij[state.idx2d(localI, j)];
                if (kij <= ZERO) continue;

                // Get radiosity from source triangle at time when emission occurred
                size_t srcTime = t - static_cast<size_t>(tauij);
                val_t radJ = state.radB[state.idxHistory(srcTime, j)];
                if (radJ <= ZERO) continue;

                // Accumulate contribution: form factor * area * source radiosity
                sumB += std::min(kij * state.areas[j], ONE) * radJ;
            }

            // Update radiosity: reflection + emission
            const val_t emission = (i == state.sourceIndex && t < state.numTimesteps / 2) ? ONE : ZERO;
            const val_t radiosity = state.rho[i] * sumB + emission;
            localCurrent[localI] = radiosity;
            state.localRadB[state.idxLocalTN(t, localI)] = radiosity;
        }

        // The next timestep needs every source triangle's current value.  A
        // row-wise allgather keeps the communication volume at O(N) per step,
        // while Kij/Tau and the target histories remain distributed.
        MPI_Allgatherv(localCurrent.data(), static_cast<int>(state.localTriangles), MPI_FLOAT,
                       state.radB.data() + state.idxHistory(t, 0),
                       state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDisplacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT,
                       state.comm);
        state.sourceRadB[t] = state.radB[state.idxHistory(t, state.sourceIndex)];

        if (state.rank == 0 && ((t + 1) % 10 == 0 || t + 1 == state.numTimesteps)) {
            printf("  Timestep %zu/%zu\n", t + 1, state.numTimesteps);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Distance Computation (Cross-Correlation)
// ============================================================================

void computeDistances(SimulationState& state) {
    if (state.rank == 0) printf("Computing distances via cross-correlation...\n");

    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangles; ++localI) {
        val_t maxCorr = ZERO;
        int bestT = 0;

        // Discrete cross-correlation to find time delay
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            val_t sum = ZERO;

            for (size_t tt = t; tt < state.numTimesteps; ++tt) {
                val_t pB = state.localRadB[state.idxLocalTN(tt, localI)];
                val_t pS = state.sourceRadB[tt - t];
                sum += pS * pB;
            }

            if (sum > maxCorr) {
                maxCorr = sum;
                bestT = static_cast<int>(t);
            }
        }

        state.localDistances[localI] = WAVE_SPEED * static_cast<val_t>(bestT);
    }

    MPI_Gatherv(state.localDistances.data(), static_cast<int>(state.localTriangles), MPI_FLOAT,
                state.rank == 0 ? state.distances.data() : nullptr,
                state.rowCounts.data(), state.rowDisplacements.data(), MPI_FLOAT,
                0, state.comm);
}

// ============================================================================
// Validation
// ============================================================================

bool validateResults(const SimulationState& state) {
    // Check the locally owned distances, then reduce validation statistics.
    bool localAllNonNegative = true;
    bool localAllFinite = true;
    val_t localMinDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    val_t localMaxDist = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t localSumDist = ZERO;
    uint64_t localNonZeroCount = 0;

    for (val_t d : state.localDistances) {
        if (d < 0) {
            localAllNonNegative = false;
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(d)) {
            localAllFinite = false;
        }
        localMinDist = std::min(localMinDist, d);
        localMaxDist = std::max(localMaxDist, d);
        localSumDist += d;
        if (d > EPSILON) ++localNonZeroCount;
    }

    val_t minDist = ZERO;
    val_t maxDist = ZERO;
    val_t sumDist = ZERO;
    uint64_t nonZeroCount = 0;
    int allNonNegative = 0;
    int allFinite = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localMinDist, &minDist, 1, MPI_FLOAT, MPI_MIN, 0, state.comm);
    MPI_Reduce(&localMaxDist, &maxDist, 1, MPI_FLOAT, MPI_MAX, 0, state.comm);
    MPI_Reduce(&localSumDist, &sumDist, 1, MPI_FLOAT, MPI_SUM, 0, state.comm);
    MPI_Reduce(&localNonZeroCount, &nonZeroCount, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0, state.comm);
    const int localNonNegativeInt = localAllNonNegative ? 1 : 0;
    const int localFiniteInt = localAllFinite ? 1 : 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localNonNegativeInt, &allNonNegative, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, 0, state.comm);
    MPI_Reduce(&localFiniteInt, &allFinite, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, 0, state.comm);

    uint64_t localReceivedEnergy = 0;
    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangles; ++localI) {
        for (size_t t = 0; t < state.numTimesteps; ++t) {
            if (state.localRadB[state.idxLocalTN(t, localI)] > EPSILON) {
                ++localReceivedEnergy;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    uint64_t receivedEnergy = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localReceivedEnergy, &receivedEnergy, 1, MPI_UINT64_T,
               MPI_SUM, 0, state.comm);

// ============================================================================
// Hash for Verification
// ============================================================================

uint64_t computeHash(const SimulationState& state) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < state.numTriangles; ++i) {
        const uint32_t* ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint32_t*>(&state.distances[i]);
        hash ^= (static_cast<uint64_t>(*ptr) + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

// ============================================================================
// Helper: Compute subdivision level from target triangle count
// ============================================================================

int getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(int targetTriangles) {
    // Icosphere: 20 triangles initially, 4x per subdivision
    // subdivisions: 0->20, 1->80, 2->320, 3->1280, 4->5120, 5->20480
    int subdivisions = 0;
    int triangles = 20;
    while (triangles < targetTriangles && subdivisions < 6) {
        subdivisions++;
        triangles *= 4;
    }
    return subdivisions;
}

// ============================================================================
// Main
// ============================================================================

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Target number of triangles (default: 320)\n");
    printf("               Actual count will be rounded to nearest icosphere level:\n");
    printf("               20, 80, 320, 1280, 5120, 20480\n");
    printf("  -t <num>     Number of timesteps (default: 50)\n");
    printf("  -s <num>     Source triangle index (default: 0)\n");
    printf("  -r <val>     Reflectivity 0.0-1.0 (default: 0.8)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -o           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

long elapsedMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {
    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
    return rank == 0 ? static_cast<long>(maximumSeconds * 1000.0) : 0;
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);

    int rank = 0;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);

    int targetTriangles = 320;
    int timesteps = 50;
    int sourceIdx = 0;
    val_t reflectivity = 0.8f;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;

    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            targetTriangles = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-t") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            timesteps = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-s") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            sourceIdx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            reflectivity = static_cast<val_t>(atof(argv[++i]));
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-o") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            if (rank == 0) printUsage(argv[0]);
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 0;
        } else {
            if (rank == 0) {
                printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
                printUsage(argv[0]);
            }
            MPI_Finalize();
            return 1;
        }
    }

    int subdivisions = getSubdivisionsForTriangleCount(targetTriangles);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Room Response Simulation Benchmark\n");
        printf("===================================\n");
        printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", SimulationState().worldSize);
        printf("Target triangles: %d (using %d subdivisions)\n", targetTriangles, subdivisions);
        printf("Timesteps: %d\n", timesteps);
        printf("Source triangle: %d\n", sourceIdx);
        printf("Reflectivity: %.2f\n", reflectivity);
        printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
        printf("\n");
    }

    // Initialize
    SimulationState state;
    initializeSimulation(state, subdivisions, static_cast<size_t>(timesteps),
                         static_cast<size_t>(sourceIdx), reflectivity);

    if (rank == 0) printf("\n");

    // Precomputation
    const double startPre = MPI_Wtime();

    computeTimeDelays(state);
    computeFormFactors(state);

    const long preDuration = elapsedMilliseconds(startPre, state.comm, state.rank);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Precomputation time: %ld ms\n", preDuration);
        printf("\n");
    }

    // Simulation
    const double startSim = MPI_Wtime();

    runSimulation(state);

    const long simDuration = elapsedMilliseconds(startSim, state.comm, state.rank);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Simulation time: %ld ms\n", simDuration);
        printf("\n");
    }

    // Distance computation
    const double startDist = MPI_Wtime();

    computeDistances(state);

    const long distDuration = elapsedMilliseconds(startDist, state.comm, state.rank);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("Distance computation time: %ld ms\n", distDuration);
        printf("\n");

    if (rank == 0) printf("Total computation time: %ld ms\n", totalTime);

    size_t n = state.numTriangles;
    size_t t = state.numTimesteps;
    double kijOps = static_cast<double>(n * n);
    double simOps = static_cast<double>(n * n * t);
    double distOps = static_cast<double>(n * t * t);

    if (rank == 0) {
        printf("\nPerformance:\n");
        printf("  Triangles: %zu\n", n);
        printf("  Timesteps: %zu\n", t);
        printf("  Form factor computations: %.2e\n", kijOps);
        printf("  Simulation operations: %.2e\n", simOps);
        printf("  Distance computations: %.2e\n", distOps);
        printf("  Total time per triangle: %.4f ms\n", static_cast<double>(totalTime) / n);
    }

    // Memory usage
    size_t memKij = state.localTriangles * n * sizeof(val_t);

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+long elapsedMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+long elapsedMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    return rank == 0 ? static_cast<long>(maximumSeconds * 1000.0) : 0;
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}
+

codex
The MPI data layout and collectives are now in place, including rank-zero-only reporting and global validation reductions. I also retained the original random-ray stream by assigning each rank the corresponding RNG offset, so a one-rank run can be compared directly with the sequential implementation. Next I’m compiling and checking hashes/results across one and multiple ranks.

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}
+

 // ============================================================================
 
 void computeFormFactors(SimulationState& state) {
-    printf("Computing form factors (Kij)...\n");
+    if (state.rank == 0) {
+        printf("Computing form factors (Kij) with %d MPI ranks...\n", state.worldSize);
+    }
+
+    // Count the pairs which consume random numbers.  An exclusive scan gives
+    // every rank its exact offset in the serial RNG stream, preserving the
+    // original ray samples while distributing the expensive visibility work.
+    uint64_t localRandomPairs = 0;
+    for (size_t localI = 0; localI < state.localTriangles; ++localI) {
+        const size_t i = state.firstTriangle + localI;
+        for (size_t j = 0; j < state.numTriangles; ++j) {
+            if (i != j && state.triangles[i].normal().dot(state.triangles[j].normal()) <= 0.99f) {
+                ++localRandomPairs;
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    uint64_t randomPairOffset = 0;
+    MPI_Exscan(&localRandomPairs, &randomPairOffset, 1, MPI_UINT64_T,
+               MPI_SUM, state.comm);
+    if (state.rank == 0) randomPairOffset = 0;

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;

+    return rank == 0 ? static_cast<long>(maximumSeconds * 1000.0) : 0;

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}
+

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}
+

+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+long elapsedMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    return rank == 0 ? static_cast<long>(maximumSeconds * 1000.0) : 0;
+}
+

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+long elapsedMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    return rank == 0 ? static_cast<long>(maximumSeconds * 1000.0) : 0;
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+long elapsedMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}
+

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+long elapsedMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    return rank == 0 ? static_cast<long>(maximumSeconds * 1000.0) : 0;
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+long elapsedMilliseconds(double start, MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {
+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);
+    return rank == 0 ? static_cast<long>(maximumSeconds * 1000.0) : 0;
+}

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}
+

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_mpi_r4/roomsim/roomsim.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_mpi_r4/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt)

Key points:

- MPI is unconditionally initialized and required by CMake.
- Form-factor/time-delay rows are distributed across ranks.
- Radiosity updates use MPI row-wise `Allgatherv`.
- Distance results and validation use MPI reductions/gathering.
- Rank-zero-only output avoids interleaved logs.
- Original RNG stream and result hashes are preserved.

Verified with MPI runs using 1, 2, 4, and 24 ranks. Results matched the sequential implementation exactly, and validation passed. The executable remains `roomsim`.

+void buildRowPartition(size_t numRows, int worldSize,
+                       std::vector<int>& counts,
+                       std::vector<int>& displacements) {
+    counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+    displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(worldSize));
+
+    const size_t base = numRows / static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    const size_t remainder = numRows % static_cast<size_t>(worldSize);
+    size_t displacement = 0;
+    for (int rank = 0; rank < worldSize; ++rank) {
+        const size_t count = base + (static_cast<size_t>(rank) < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+        if (count > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+            displacement > static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+            fprintf(stderr, "MPI row partition exceeds the supported count range\n");
+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);
+        }
+        counts[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(count);
+        displacements[static_cast<size_t>(rank)] = static_cast<int>(displacement);
+        displacement += count;
+    }
+}

+    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double maximumSeconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &maximumSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0, comm);

Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism in:

- [roomsim.cpp](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_mpi_r4/roomsim/roomsim.cpp)
- [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/roomsim_gpt-5.6-luna-xhigh_mpi_r4/roomsim/CMakeLists.txt)

Key points:

- MPI is unconditionally initialized and required by CMake.
- Form-factor/time-delay rows are distributed across ranks.
- Radiosity updates use MPI row-wise `Allgatherv`.
- Distance results and validation use MPI reductions/gathering.
- Rank-zero-only output avoids interleaved logs.
- Original RNG stream and result hashes are preserved.

Verified with MPI runs using 1, 2, 4, and 24 ranks. Results matched the sequential implementation exactly, and validation passed. The executable remains `roomsim`.
